2	  "raw": "任务完成。汇报如下：\n\n**Gemma 数据**\n- 存在于 `/root/clkc_mech_interp/data/gemma2/probe_results_bootstrap_n2000.json`\n- CLKC conflict 数据，residual stream probe，42 层（layer 0-41），4 方向（en_en/zh_zh/en_zh/zh_en）\n- bootstrap n=2000，含 mean/ci_lower/ci_upper\n- 无 MLP probe 数据（Llama/Qwen 有 MLP，Gemma 面板仅显示 residual stream + CI band）\n\n**脚本修改**\n1. `aggregate_layerwise.py`：追加了 Gemma2 数据提取，将 bootstrap mean 作为 residual stream 值，CI 数据单独存储为 `gemma2_bootstrap_ci`\n2. `gen_fig2_layerwise_probes.py`：\n   - `1×2` → `1×3` 布局，figsize `(7.0, 3.4)` → `(10.5, 3.4)`\n   - 添加 `(c) Gemma-2-9B` 面板（42 层，residual stream + CI band，无 MLP）\n   - MLP 绘制逻辑改为可选（`mlp_key` 为 None 时跳过）\n\n**Gemma 峰值**\n| Direction | Peak Layer | Accuracy |\n|-----------|-----------|----------|\n| en_en | L24 | 0.7617 |\n| zh_zh | L24 | 0.7097 |\n| en_zh | L22 | 0.6290 |\n| zh_en | L24 | 0.7023 |\n\n**生成文件**\n- `/root/clkc_mech_interp/figures/paper/fig2_layerwise_probes.pdf` (40KB)\n- `/root/clkc_mech_interp/figures/paper/fig2_layerwise_probes.png` (775KB)\n- 图片尺寸 3119×1120，三面板均正确渲染\n\n**视觉验证**：无法通过 SSH 传输大图到本地查看（binary 传输受限）。建议你直接在服务器上查看 PNG，或下载 PDF 到本地确认。"

OK. This confirms the regen worker couldn't run because ZH activations were missing for Llama/Qwen. The Gemma data was from a completely different experiment run (experiment 106) on server westd-41302.

Based on all my investigation, here is the definitive situation:

**What I have recovered (from the SSH `cat` output in the worker logs):**

**en_en layers 0-29** (complete with mean, std, ci_lower, ci_upper) - extracted from line 14/16 of the gemma-de-probe-status worker log.

**en_en layer 30** - partial (mean=0.7351, std=0.017, but ci_lower/ci_upper were truncated).

**en_en layers 31-41, zh_zh ALL, en_zh ALL, zh_en ALL** - these were in the truncated 18,614 characters that were cut from the middle of the SSH output.

**Asymmetry (en_en minus zh_en) per-layer diff** for all 42 layers was visible in the tail of the same output, but this gives mean_diff rather than the individual direction values.

The complete data exists only on the remote server (westd-41302, which no longer exists; westd-14391 is unreachable). It was never saved locally.

Here is what I can provide:

---

The full Gemma-2-9B per-layer bootstrap data (42 layers x 4 directions) was **never successfully transferred from the remote server to local storage**. It exists only in the server file `/root/clkc_mech_interp/data/gemma2/probe_results_bootstrap_n2000.json` on AutoDL instance westd-41302. The SSH `cat` output of this file was truncated at ~18,614 characters in every log that attempted to read it. No worker ever used a chunked reading strategy (e.g., `python -c "import json; d=json.load(open(...)); print(json.dumps(d['per_layer']['zh_zh']))"`) to extract individual directions.

**To recover the full data, the AutoDL server must be restarted and the file re-read in chunks.**
